[Icemule Trace, Town Center] (4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.  You also see a sharp-beaked drake verlok'ar that is flying around, a sharp-taloned dusky snow-owl that is flying around, the cupcake-like Fumbl disk crafted from pastel-colored glass, the Jonsie disk, a flying brown leather tome, the discordant Siarnaq disk, the gold-dusted Moomfs disk crowned with sylvarraend rubies, an impish snow spirit that is flying around, the enruned Vymmer disk, a wily mountain spirit that is flying around, the icy blue Besrudio disk, the orange Zinnanzi disk, a merry alpine spirit that is flying around, an herb-carved walnut table with some stuff on it, a blue-eyed charcoal cat and a woven bark wastebin.

Occasional cheers of victory or groans of failure echo from the west, where the rooftop of the town's gaming hall glistens with a fresh tracing of silvery ice.  Several newcomers to town, standing out due to their foreign garb and lack of layered clothing, filter through the doors of a stone cottage.  A few crafty young krolvin linger outside, plying them for spare silvers.
Obvious paths: north, east, south, west

>
Halfling and giantkin vendors hawk their wares along the sides of the busy thoroughfare, the former cohort frequently standing atop crates and soapboxes so as not to be lost in the crowd.  A troop of half-krolvin trappers makes their way through the throng, coins jingling in their pockets.  Though drifts of fresh-fallen snow dot the roadside, a wrought iron street lamp at the corner stands near a cleared path, occasionally serving as a resting post for weary travelers.
Obvious paths: north, south

[Icemule Trace, South Road - 2412] (u4042225)
A flow of adventurers, merchants, trappers, and lumberjacks congeals into a noisy throng near the South Gate of Icemule Trace.  Adding to the clack of wagon wheels and the din of the crowd, sounds of laughter and clanking mugs beckon from the inns and taverns that occupy the Tavern Burrow, just to the west.   The road lies in the shadow of the town's high walls and barbican towers, the foundations of which are of darker stone than the top.  You also see a green-eyed sable tiger.
Obvious paths: north, east, west
Your drake verlok'ar followed.

[South Barbican, Gate - 2486] (u4050101)
Under the massive barbican is a shadowy passageway, with sturdy stone walls on each side and overhead.  Iron-barred windows provide access to this area from each of the barbican towers as well as from overhead, creating the distinct impression that someone is watching from the shadows.  The sharp spikes of raised portcullises jut from the ceiling at the northern entrance to the city and the southern exit to the wilds.  You also see a silver frost-covered wagon and a rotund coachman.
Obvious exits: north, south
Your drake verlok'ar followed.
[go2]>south

[Icemule Trace, South Gate - 2487] (u4128001)
Thick stone walls frame a wide, wooden gate.  A vaulted stone archway provides some cover from the weather, which is often inclement so close to the glaciers.  The packed earth pathway skirting the town's walls gives way to a cobbled street in the same dark grey stone as the lower half of the walls.  You also see Guard Sergeant Smiley, a snow-capped pine tree, the South Gate and a snowbank.
Obvious paths: east, west
Your drake verlok'ar followed.

[Commerce Burrow, Trader Way - 2423] (u4042306)
Peddlers from out of Briarmoon Cove and Pinefar wander up and down the road, loudly touting their wares.  A general store sits to the side of the road, decorated with banners in a cheerful rainbow of colors.  The customers are an eclectic mix of fledgling adventurers and townsfolk, the two groups united in their need for various odds and ends.  You also see a soft grey penguin feather and the corpse of a halfling bandit.

[Icemule Trace, East Road - 2426] (u4042301)
A pair of town guards are stationed outside the archway leading into the town's bank.  One halfling guard seems to be dozing on his stool, while the other keeps a close and wary eye on the foot traffic flowing past.  The bank is a tall building, its clapboard exterior painted a bright wintry blue that complements its dark slate-shingled roof.  Several krolvin sailors count their money nearby, using the top of an old oaken hogshead to stack coins familiar and strange.  You also see a chunk of firewood.
Obvious paths: east, south, west
Your drake verlok'ar followed.

[Icemule Trace, East Road - 2427] (u4042302)
As it passes by a half-timbered pale grey stone guildhall, the straight march of the cobblestone road offers a view of snowy foothills beyond the town's walls.  Over the heads of fur-clad merchants from Pinefar and robed sisters leading groups of fresh-faced initiates out of town, the snow-swept wilds are dotted with evergreens and dark rock.  Glimpses of a pale stone abbey in the extreme distance are almost entirely lost to the cloud cover.  To the south, the slate-shingled roof of Clovertooth Hall rises high into the sky.
Obvious paths: east, south, west
Your drake verlok'ar followed.
[go2]>go guildhall

[Adventurer's Guild, Lobby - 3778] (u15004001)
Complex layers of stacked stone, ranging in color from palest grey to nearly solid black, form the entire rear wall into one massive fireplace.  The flames spread their welcome warmth throughout the greatroom, warding off the chill let in by the constant comings and goings of guild members.  Enormous tusked and horned heads of the fierce beasts of the north are mounted along the east wall, gazing at the rest of the guild through glass eyes.  You also see Guild Taskmaster Halline and a basket of sticks.

Glimpses of a pale stone abbey in the extreme distance are almost entirely lost to the cloud cover.  To the south, the slate-shingled roof of Clovertooth Hall rises high into the sky.
Obvious paths: east, south, west
Your drake verlok'ar followed.
[go2]>west
>
[Icemule Trace, East Road - 2426] (u4042301)
A pair of town guards are stationed outside the archway leading into the town's bank.  One halfling guard seems to be dozing on his stool, while the other keeps a close and wary eye on the foot traffic flowing past.  The bank is a tall building, its clapboard exterior painted a bright wintry blue that complements its dark slate-shingled roof.  Several krolvin sailors count their money nearby, using the top of an old oaken hogshead to stack coins familiar and strange.  You also see a chunk of firewood.
Obvious paths: east, south, west
Your drake verlok'ar followed.
[go2]>south

[Commerce Burrow, Trader Way - 2423] (u4042306)
Peddlers from out of Briarmoon Cove and Pinefar wander up and down the road, loudly touting their wares.  A general store sits to the side of the road, decorated with banners in a cheerful rainbow of colors.  The customers are an eclectic mix of fledgling adventurers and townsfolk, the two groups united in their need for various odds and ends.  You also see the burly giantman Sulliver, a soft grey penguin feather and the corpse of a halfling bandit.

Halfling and giantkin vendors hawk their wares along the sides of the busy thoroughfare, the former cohort frequently standing atop crates and soapboxes so as not to be lost in the crowd.  A troop of half-krolvin trappers makes their way through the throng, coins jingling in their pockets.  Though drifts of fresh-fallen snow dot the roadside, a wrought iron street lamp at the corner stands near a cleared path, occasionally serving as a resting post for weary travelers.

Under the massive barbican is a shadowy passageway, with sturdy stone walls on each side and overhead.  Iron-barred windows provide access to this area from each of the barbican towers as well as from overhead, creating the distinct impression that someone is watching from the shadows.  The sharp spikes of raised portcullises jut from the ceiling at the northern entrance to the city and the southern exit to the wilds.  You also see a silver frost-covered wagon and a rotund coachman.

As it passes by a half-timbered pale grey stone guildhall, the straight march of the cobblestone road offers a view of snowy foothills beyond the town's walls.  Over the heads of fur-clad merchants from Pinefar and robed sisters leading groups of fresh-faced initiates out of town, the snow-swept wilds are dotted with evergreens and dark rock.  Glimpses of a pale stone abbey in the extreme distance are almost entirely lost to the cloud cover.  To the south, the slate-shingled roof of Clovertooth Hall rises high into the sky.

[Icemule Trace, Town Center - 2300] (u4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.
